### SSBO allocation contract parsing — 2026-10-22

- Added `ShaderPackStorageBufferProperties` for active, preprocessed Iris `bufferObject.0` through `.8` declarations. It parses fixed byte sizes and the documented screen-relative form, resolves relative sizes with Iris's width/height scaling formula, and rejects malformed values, overflow, and VulkanMod's signed-int descriptor-range limit. `ShaderPack.readStorageBufferProperties()` uses the selected profile's processed `shaders.properties`.
- The active post-processing SSBO guard now reports the block name, binding, and resolved allocation size (or the missing/invalid allocation) before VulkanMod's parser runs. The active block is still rejected: this change does **not** allocate Vulkan storage buffers, bind storage descriptors, or add the shadow/compute/post memory barriers needed for correct data visibility.
- This follows Iris's `bufferObject` syntax and size rules in its [SSBO reference](https://github.com/IrisShaders/ShaderDoc/blob/master/iris-features.md#shader-storage-buffer-objects). The pinned Complementary pack has profile-conditional slot 0 sizes from 50,855,936 through 810,549,248 bytes and also declares slot 3 at 5,376 bytes; the selected runtime profile is `COMPLEMENTARY`.
- Full forced JDK 21 `test validateShaders build packageExamplePacks compileGraphicsSmokeJava --rerun-tasks --no-daemon` passed: **436 tests, 0 failures/errors**, 96 example shaders preprocessed and 112 direct SPIR-V compilations. `scripts/verify_jar.py` passed for the unpublished beta.30-metadata candidate JAR (**888,706 bytes**, SHA-256 `9dcdca33787329718509606ca952d6dddd41f07d24dbbe37a69255ab53bdfbdb`). No new real-pack GPU run or pinned source audit was performed for this parsing-only change.
- PR #84 remains draft. Keep the active `blockDataBuffer` refusal until GPU allocation, per-pipeline storage descriptors, resource lifetime, barriers, and real Complementary validation are implemented. No merge or release is claimed.

### Complementary shader-source scanning follow-up — 2026-10-22

- Replaced the comma-separated entity varying expansion regex with a linear token parser, including multiline declarations, to prevent render-thread backtracking while applying the real pack profile. Replaced the unused-varying declaration regex with a line/token parser for the same reason. `EntityVertexShaderAdapter.extractOutputs` now reuses its already-cleaned source for unsupported-output checks instead of re-masking and rescanning the whole shader.
- Added regression coverage for multiline varying declarations and long lists, multiline layout/qualifier syntax, used-vs-unused inputs, and thousands of declarations. The targeted adapter tests passed under JDK 21.
- Replaced per-uniform declaration regex recompilation and backtracking with cached, possessive declaration patterns plus an identifier precheck. Replaced per-name whole-source regex substitution for linked entity varyings with one token pass. Targeted tests passed: 193 tests across seven adapter classes, including multiline uniform declarations and the maximum supported eight custom entity varyings.
- The RTX 3070 Complementary Reimagined `COMPLEMENTARY` run initially crashed in `SkyRenderer.renderMoon` after 87 client ticks: `VkGpuDevice` received a null shader source because `purgeUnusedPipelines()` retained only default sky pipelines and pruned SUN/MOON/VOID stage variants. `SkyPipelineSet.allPipelines()` now includes its stage variants in the retained pipeline set, so their cached shader sources survive until profile replacement.
- The full forced JDK 21 `test validateShaders build packageExamplePacks compileGraphicsSmokeJava --rerun-tasks --no-daemon` now passes: **475 tests, 0 failures/errors**, 96 example shaders preprocessed and 112 directly compiled to Vulkan SPIR-V. ### Vulkan feature и прямые SSBO компиляции — 2026-10-22

- Проверка failed Graphics smoke для `f3bdcf1` подтвердила два runtime-дефекта с Vulkan validation layer: 8 `VUID-RuntimeSpirv-NonWritable-06341` в entity/custom-image сценариях (feature `vertexPipelineStoresAndAtomics` не считался включённым) и 6 `VUID-VkGraphicsPipelineCreateInfo-layout-07990` в `storage-buffer-shadow` (SPIR-V vertex SSBO был на binding 0, где layout содержал `UNIFORM_BUFFER_DYNAMIC`). Остальные задания этой матрицы прошли.
- Перевёл запрос поддерживаемых core features на `VkPhysicalDeviceFeatures2` в `VkDeviceCreateInfo.pNext`, обнуляя `pEnabledFeatures` и сохраняя исходную VulkanMod-цепочку `VkPhysicalDeviceVulkan11Features`. Добавил regression-тест на поля features2 и сохранность цепочки. Khronos описывает features2 в device-create chain как источник core feature requests вместо `pEnabledFeatures`: https://registry.khronos.org/vulkan/specs/latest/man/html/VkDeviceCreateInfo.html.
- Подтверждённый обход parser bridge: `Pipeline.Builder.createGraphicsPipeline()` VulkanMod компилирует terrain/shadow/cloud исходники напрямую через shaderc; compute также компилируется напрямую. Добавил direct-source подготовку активных SSBO: binding `N` становится `1024 + N`, а для активного `bufferObject.N` добавляется `VeyraStorageBufferUBO` с нужными shader-stage flags. Подключил её к terrain, shadow-terrain, cloud и compute прямым компиляциям. Parser-driven native render pipelines продолжают пользоваться прежним bridge.
- Добавил регрессионную проверку direct-source binding. Полный JDK 21 прогон `test validateShaders build packageExamplePacks compileGraphicsSmokeJava --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain` завершился успешно: **481 тест, 0 failures/errors/skips**, 96 example shader sources проверены, 112 скомпилированы в Vulkan SPIR-V, пример-паки упакованы.

### Complementary Unbound textures and transition-safe shadows — 2026-10-22

- Added shader-pack texture support for both global `customTexture.*` aliases and program/stage-scoped `texture.<stage>.<sampler>` entries on supported terrain/shadow geometry pipelines. `gbuffers` mappings also apply to shadow geometry as Iris does. Pack PNG assets are loaded through the existing path-safe shader reader, cached by pack/resource, uploaded to Vulkan, and use `.mcmeta` blur/clamp settings. This resolves Complementary Unbound's unsupported `gaux4` input from `texture.gbuffers.gaux4`.
- The first real Unbound lifecycle run exposed a second transition crash: after resource/dimension changes, a geometry pass could request `shadowcolor0` before Vulkan had recorded a completed shadow map. Veyra now tracks map readiness, binds a neutral 1×1 texture during that gap, and skips post passes that require an unavailable shadow map. The next recorded shadow pass restores normal sampling.
- RTX 3070 smoke `run-graphics-complementary-unbound-profile-smoke-retry5-20261022` passed with profile `COMPLEMENTARY`: **360,000 non-black pixels / 54 color buckets**, all lifecycle flags true (resize, Nether, return, disable, re-enable, reload), **810,549,248-byte** SSBO, and an empty error field. The client log contains no `shadowcolor0` crash, `VUID`, Vulkan validation error, or device-lost marker. The host has no Vulkan validation layer; this is a selected-profile synthetic-world lifecycle check, not whole-pack parity.

### Iris render-relative shadow compute sizing — 2026-10-22

- Continued from beta.31 candidate head `4e017be` on isolated branch `feature/compute-render-size`; kept PR #85's branch and its active watcher untouched.
- Added Iris `const vec2 workGroupsRender` parsing, including its default `(1, 1)` sizing when neither dispatch form is declared. Dispatch groups are resolved from the current main render target each frame, using Iris's `float` scale multiplication, rounded pixel extent, and local-size division. Zero-sized work is skipped, and compute barriers are emitted only between stages that actually dispatch.
- Kept literal `const ivec3 workGroups` support, rejected conflicting/negative sizing declarations, and updated the `custom-image-compute` example to use render-relative sizing with an image-bounds guard.
- Verified the implementation against Iris's [compute directive parser](https://github.com/IrisShaders/Iris/blob/26.1/common/src/main/java/net/irisshaders/iris/shaderpack/parsing/ComputeDirectiveParser.java), [compute dispatch](https://github.com/IrisShaders/Iris/blob/26.1/common/src/main/java/net/irisshaders/iris/gl/program/ComputeProgram.java), and [compute pass documentation](https://github.com/IrisShaders/ShaderDoc/blob/master/passes/compute.md).

Targeted `ComputeShaderAdapterTest` passed. ### Iris indirect shadow-compute dispatch — 2026-10-22

- Continued the existing PR #86 stack on `feature/compute-render-size`; preserved the beta.31 base and the separate root checkout's local changes.
- Implemented Iris `indirect.<pass> = <bufferObjectNumber> <offsetInBuffer>` for active `shadowcomp` compute stages. Parsing validates pass names, buffer slots, non-negative four-byte-aligned offsets, and that the complete 12-byte `VkDispatchIndirectCommand` fits the selected fixed or screen-relative SSBO allocation. Indirect buffers now request `VK_BUFFER_USAGE_INDIRECT_BUFFER_BIT`; the pipeline records `vkCmdDispatchIndirect` and inserts shader-write to indirect-read barriers between stages and before following work.
- Added the `shadowcomp_a.csh` producer/consumer fixture: `shadowcomp.csh` writes one indirect dispatch command and an intermediate value; `shadowcomp_a.csh` consumes the command and completes the shared 3D image. The smoke signal is 42 only when both compute stages execute. `SSBO` is now recognized in `iris.features.required`; unsupported image/compute requirements remain gated by the existing capability checks.
- Extended the CPU shader smoke to compile both compute stages and run the producer through the same SSBO source preparation used by the Vulkan runtime; the indirect fixture passed shaderc/SPIR-V compilation.
- Added unit coverage for indirect parsing, fixed and relative buffer bounds, missing allocation, malformed binding/offset, and required-SSBO capability recognition. Targeted storage-buffer/options tests passed. Full forced JDK 21 gate `test validateShaders build packageExamplePacks compileGraphicsSmokeJava --rerun-tasks --no-daemon --console=plain` passed: **511 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips**, 97 example shaders processed, 113 direct Vulkan SPIR-V compilations, graphics-smoke Java compilation, build, and example-pack packaging. `git diff --check` passed.

The terrain signal requires computed value 42, so it confirms the indirect consumer stage ran. The Windows host has no Vulkan validation layer; this local run does not replace Lavapipe validation.

### #86 hosted Graphics: textured-default fixture after terrain fallback — 2026-10-23

- #86 Graphics `39909787743` on `3943bdb`: **44/45 jobs passed**. Build and the pinned source audit passed. `textured-default-vertex-attributes` failed with 0 magenta pixels. It had failed the same way on every earlier #86 head (`e15c502`, `9151793`) but passed on #85.
- Cause: #86 correctly applies Iris' terrain fallback chain (`gbuffers_terrain` → `gbuffers_textured`). The fixture's `gbuffers_textured` therefore also drew terrain, where real `mc_Entity`/`mc_midTexCoord` values trigger its discard. The terrain disappeared, and the additive world-border signal landed on bright sky as pale pink. Reproduced on the RTX 3070.
- Fix: the fixture now provides a solid `gbuffers_terrain`, so the opaque background remains. No renderer change. Local RTX 3070 rerun passed with **144,348 active magenta pixels**. The local gate passed again.

### Исправление shadow-прохода и повторный GPU smoke — 2026-10-24

- Удалены четыре безусловных исключения при привязке shadow color/depth во время записи shadow map. При `shadowMapCompleted=false` binder-ы используют уже подготовленный neutral fallback; вызовы и владельцы ресурсов не менялись. Изменение зафиксировано в `0eee746` и отправлено в PR #112.
- Полный JDK 21 gate на исходниках PR #112: 582 теста, 0 failures/errors/skips; build, shader validation (104 примера / 122 direct Vulkan SPIR-V), graphics-smoke compilation и упаковка example packs прошли.
- RTX 3070, Complementary Reimagined default: smoke `passed`, 230 400 активных пикселей, 48 color buckets, ошибка пустая. Sildur Vibrant lifecycle: `passed`, 360 000 пикселей / 58 buckets; resize, Nether, возврат, disable, re-enable и reload подтверждены.
- Эти результаты подтверждают работу smoke-сцен и lifecycle, но не визуальный parity: контролируемая синтетическая сцена показывает значительный visual parity-дефект: Veyra темнее, без облаков, а стена визуально расходится с Iris; natural-world проверка остаётся открытой. Причина не установлена; natural-world сравнение и стабильная совместимость не подтверждены.
- Исправление ещё не перенесено в локальную beta.42 release-prep ветку, основанную на прежнем head PR #112. Перед выпуском обновить её после merge и повторить версионированный gate/JAR verifier. Hosted checks нового PR head ожидаются через T3 watcher; вручную их не опрашивали.

### Shadow block storage-image routing validation - 2026-10-26

- On exact `origin/main` `72de9312c8170739f1a1d660d57ea135cc79b373`, traced the Complementary Unbound `COMPLEMENTARY` failure to the graphics storage-image stage guard: `shadow_entities.vsh` was allowed, while the shared shadow vertex source selected for `shadow_block` was rejected. The guard now permits writable custom images only for `shadow_entities.vsh` and `shadow_block.vsh`; fragment, terrain shadow, and ordinary scene stages remain rejected. Added a focused policy regression covering both allowed stages and four forbidden cases.
- Focused `ShaderPackImageBindingsTest` passed. Full JDK 21 gate `test validateShaders build compileGraphicsSmokeJava packageExamplePacks --rerun-tasks --no-daemon` passed: 598 tests (0 failures/errors/skips), 104 example shaders, and 122 Vulkan SPIR-V modules. The produced beta.48 JAR passed `scripts/verify_jar.py` for Minecraft 1.21.11 / VulkanMod 0.6.8.
- RTX 3070 GraphicsSmoke on the pinned Complementary Unbound r5.9.3 archive (SHA-512 matches `validation/shaderpack-inputs.json`), profile `COMPLEMENTARY`, completed with `status=passed`, 360,000 non-black pixels, 130 color buckets, and an empty error field. Resize, Nether, return to Overworld, disable, re-enable, and real resource reload all passed. The run loaded 14 entity shadow pipelines and 19 block-entity/sign-text pipelines; no `shadow_block` load rejection or `cannot write graphics storage image` error appeared. Result: `veyra-entity-shadow-mv-parity-20261026/build/reports/unbound-shadow-block-storage-20261026/result.json`.
- The run used the local NVIDIA driver without Khronos Vulkan validation. This verifies the reported Unbound lifecycle path; it does not close the separate natural entity-shadow parity or Sildur sign-glyph readability gaps. No new release was cut; beta.49 remains the latest published release until those compatibility limitations are addressed and release criteria are met.
- Implementation branch `fix/shadow-block-storage-image-20261026` is based on `72de931`; PR #138 is open at head `4c6a0e78d2598f4e2a4be6e37d99e77e1c869711` and linked to this thread. Hosted exact-head checks are pending.

### Shadow texture coordinates and comparison reference — 2026-10-26

- Found the missing natural entity-shadow cause: VulkanMod records a negative-height viewport, while shader-pack shadow vertices retained OpenGL clip Y. The produced image was vertically mirrored relative to the UVs used by pack receivers. Terrain/entity pack wrappers now invert clip Y after pack execution; the native fallback shadow projection performs the same conversion. Shadow rasterization compensates face winding so the coordinate conversion preserves facing semantics.
- Removed the adapter-added 0.0005 comparison-reference bias from terrain, entity, textured and post paths. The shader pack supplies its own depth reference; emulated GL_LEQUAL now compares that reference directly. Removing bias alone did not fix the cast shadow; the Y conversion did.
- Full JDK 21 gate passed: 600 tests, 104 example shaders, 122 Vulkan SPIR-V modules, build, GraphicsSmoke compilation and example packaging. RTX 3070 Complementary Reimagined HIGH passed with 230,400 non-black pixels / 142 color buckets and no reported errors. In the matched golem-shadow ROI, mean luminance is 112.68 versus Iris 112.04; MAE fell from 22.67 to 5.33, and pixels where Iris is darker by >20 fell from 3,746 to 61. The long cast shadow is visible. This is generated-scene evidence, not full natural-world parity.
- Strengthened `shadow-color-entity` to require both shadow-color aliases and near depth in its expected OpenGL UV quadrant. On unmodified PR #140 it failed (active marker=0, disabled=0); on this candidate it passed (active=8,788, disabled=0).
- Pinned source audit passed: four packs, three dimensions, 12 defaults / 63 profile cases; 939 geometry fragments accepted, none rejected; all 18 active compute stages compiled.
- PR #140's cancelled Graphics job was retried successfully. Build, source audit and Graphics runs all passed; there were no review comments. Merged with expected head `4f8d01f`, producing main `9a1638e`. This candidate was fast-forwarded onto that main without changing its source tree. Further pack lifecycle checks and hosted candidate checks remain before release.
### 2026-10-27: preserve final-pass scene depth
- The 1.21.11 GameRenderer clears the main depth before the hand and again before GUI extraction. Preserve world depth for the hand when Veyra is enabled and execute the composite before the GUI clear, so final shaders can sample both world and hand depth. Vanilla depth clearing remains active when Veyra is disabled.
- RTX tests: final world-depth fixture baseline 0/0 marker pixels, candidate 222865/0; final hand-depth fixture baseline 0/0, candidate 48382/0; visible HUD and a fallback hand near a wall were checked against baseline. Vanilla-baseline and full Gradle gate passed. Source audit: four pinned packs, 939 geometry programs and 18 compute stages accepted.
- Full RTX lifecycles passed Complementary HIGH 360000/139, BSL HIGH 360000/128, Sildur default 360000/154. Unbound COMPLEMENTARY is still running. These observations do not cover every hand or UI scenario; hosted CI remains pending.

### 2026-10-27: post-pass projection parity
- Post-pass projection uniforms now use OpenGL clip conventions while geometry draw projections stay unchanged. VulkanMod's infinite-style depth coefficient is moved to the nearest finite representable value below -1 for these uniforms, preventing negative homogeneous w for sky rays. At 128 blocks the tested reconstruction error is below one block; this remains an approximation at larger distances.
- The geometry-view-space deferred fixture compares reconstructed depth against the terrain view position and checks positive sky-ray w. RTX baseline `a12d5c4` failed (0 active / 0 disabled), candidate passed (222359 / 0). Matched Sildur floor ROIs improved from RGB MAE 90.41 to 10.59 against Iris, and Complementary/BSL sky detail returned.
- Full Gradle gate and pinned four-pack audit passed; final candidate RTX lifecycles passed Complementary HIGH 360000/131, BSL HIGH 360000/127, Sildur default 360000/158, and Unbound COMPLEMENTARY 360000/121 with storage-buffer recreation. GUI-stage depth preservation is being investigated separately and is not included here. Hosted CI remains pending.
### 2026-10-27: fluid vertex normals
- VulkanMod passes zero packed normals for fluid vertices. Reconstruct missing normals from quad geometry while preserving supplied normals; reuse the same normal for tangent construction.
- RTX water-block-id paired regression: baseline `a12d5c4` (tree identical to `222fac2`) failed with 0 active / 0 disabled marker pixels; candidate passed with 226902 / 0. Complementary HIGH water was also visible in the matched basin scene. Full Gradle build, tests, shader validation, graphics-smoke compilation and example packaging passed.
- Sildur reflection parity and full release remain open. Pinned shaderpack source audit passed: 4 packs, 3 dimensions, 63 profile cases, 939 accepted geometry programs and 18 compiled compute stages. Hosted CI remains pending.

The direct sign probe isolates the needed screen-to-view behavior but does not justify changing all fragment-coordinate reads. Testing whether the geometry-stage `gbufferProjectionInverse` uniform should use the existing OpenGL-converted projection inverse instead; no fix is accepted yet.

### 2026-11-02: RTX runtime regression investigation

- A verified detached worktree at exact 0.2.1 (`75bb6bc`) passed the full Complementary Reimagined r5.9.3 HIGH lifecycle on the RTX 3070: 360,000 non-black pixels, 137 color buckets, and successful resize, Nether, return, disable, re-enable, and reload. The initial 0.2.2 HIGH capture failed at stage 0 with 20,879 non-black pixels and 12 color buckets.
- An isolated 0.2.2 ablation passed when only mipmap requests were disabled (230,400 pixels / 135 buckets); keeping the snapshot path while removing the custom sampler override still failed. Minecraft 1.21.11 `PostPass` bytecode confirmed that it appends `Sampler` to `Input.samplerName()`. The mipmapped input had passed already-suffixed names, producing incorrect descriptor names. Changing those inputs to `In` and `VeyraColortexN` fixed the isolated 0.2.2 initial capture (230,400 / 135, empty error).
- The two-name fix is in the active release branch. It corrected the custom `PostPass.Input` sampler bindings: `In` and `VeyraColortexN` are now passed as input names, with `PostPass` adding the `Sampler` suffix. The corrected 0.2.2 code passed the complete RTX 3070 Complementary HIGH lifecycle: 360,000 non-black pixels / 137 color buckets; resize, Nether, return, disable, re-enable and reload all passed, with empty error.
- On Temurin JDK 21.0.12.1, the corrected tree passed `test validateShaders packageExamplePacks compileGraphicsSmokeJava build --rerun-tasks`: 622 JUnit tests, 0 failures/errors/skips; 104 example shaders and 122 direct Vulkan SPIR-V compilations; example-pack packaging and GraphicsSmoke compilation passed. The 5 Python tooling tests passed. `scripts/verify_jar.py` accepted the local fixed candidate JAR for Minecraft 1.21.11 / VulkanMod 0.6.8 / Veyra 0.2.2+mc1.21.11. Candidate size: 1,079,351 bytes; SHA-256 `31015E6BC8804170DE9F0C5609E70F6E1D903D031B42B76B1DA5932DABE83861`.
- The published v0.2.2 tag and 1,079,322-byte JAR still refer to the unfixed release commit; the tag and published asset will not be rewritten. Ship the correction as v0.2.3 after the stacked branches merge, then verify its hosted release asset and checksum.
- Pushing another commit to the already-tagged v0.2.2 release branch will make its `Release` push workflow reject the moved branch head by design: the workflow requires the tag commit to equal the pushed SHA. The release asset already published at that tag remains independently verifiable.

## Archived multiversion investigation notes (2026-11-09)

Historical notes below describe intermediate states and are superseded by the newest entries above.

# Minecraft 1.21.x porting status

This is an implementation tracker, not a list of supported releases. The published Veyra build still targets **1.21.11 only**.

## Dependency inventory

VulkanMod releases advertised by the [Modrinth version API](https://api.modrinth.com/v2/project/JYQhtZtO/version), checked 2026-11-08:

| Minecraft | VulkanMod candidate | Modrinth version ID | Veyra status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1.21 / 1.21.1 | 0.6.7+1.21.1 | `2eqroDKD` | Port required |
| 1.21.2 / 1.21.3 | 0.5.3 | `mr9uxFGT` | Port required |
| 1.21.4 | 0.5.6 | `RAtVo03C` | Port required |
| 1.21.5 | 0.5.6 | `7pYvUnJk` | Full build, 707 JUnit tests, shader compilation and four GPU fixtures pass; cloud, animated atlas and water/shadow checks still pending |
| 1.21.6 / 1.21.7 / 1.21.8 | No advertised release | — | Renderer dependency port required |
| 1.21.9 / 1.21.10 | 0.6.6 | `jkDMvvWU` | Builds and six representative GPU fixtures pass; full pack coverage pending |
| 1.21.11 | 0.6.8+1.21.11 | `hWKRTXbQ` | Existing supported target |

An absent advertised release does not establish that an upstream source port is impossible. Full coverage includes resolving the renderer dependency for those versions.

## Reproduce the first port probe

Use JDK 21:

```sh
./gradlew --project-cache-dir .gradle/targets/1.21.10 compileJava -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.10
```

PowerShell requires quoting the property argument:

```powershell
.\gradlew.bat --project-cache-dir .gradle/targets/1.21.10 compileJava '-PtargetMinecraft=1.21.10'
```

Pinned target properties live in `gradle/targets/`. Their outputs are isolated under `build/targets/<version>/`. Without a target argument the existing 1.21.11 defaults apply. Generated mod metadata uses the chosen Minecraft and VulkanMod versions; this does not establish runtime compatibility.

Each port needs compilation, applicable regression and shader tests, distributable verification, mixin application checks, and graphics lifecycle/pack validation before it can be marked supported. Build CI and the JAR verifier cover 1.21.9/10/11; published releases still target 1.21.11.

Use a distinct `--project-cache-dir` for every target, including `.gradle/targets/1.21.11` for default builds when switching versions. Sharing project task history can make Gradle delete another target's output as stale, even with separate build directories. Set this on the command line; changing it in `settings.gradle` is too late. CI supplies the flag for each matrix entry.

## First compilation findings

The 1.21.10 probe resolves and remaps its pinned dependencies. Shared-source name adaptation covers `Identifier` to `ResourceLocation`, the old `RenderType` package, `Util`, and the `BiFunction` shader-source callback. Generated sources are rebuilt when the adaptation rules change.

Compilation still reports 11 errors (plus two mixin warnings). Remaining work includes the old composite render-state API, texture-owned filtering instead of `GpuSampler`, environment uniforms before the environment-attribute API, and changed injection signatures. The compiler has not yet type-checked all method bodies, so this count is not an estimate of the full remaining port.

No injection has been disabled to make the probe pass. Graphics validation and distribution checks remain outstanding for this target.

The first legacy source overlays now read animated albedo frame/sub-frame state from the actual `TextureAtlasSprite` ticker wrappers and `SpriteContents.Ticker`, rather than trying to access the 1.21.11 animation-state list. Older unpadded atlas sprites report zero padding. Their mixin annotation processing passes during the still-failing full compile; runtime application remains unverified.

## Legacy texture binding adapter

The 1.21.10 sources now translate independent sampler settings into the old texture-owned address/filter/mipmap state and call `RenderPass.bindSampler`. Texture-bind hooks retain noise repeat, post mipmaps, companion material bindings, and depth snapshot descriptors. Native sampler lifecycle calls become value-only operations because the old API allocates samplers through the texture.

Run the CPU contract check with:

```powershell
.\gradlew.bat checkPortAdapters '-PtargetMinecraft=1.21.10'
```

This checks state application before binding, separate U/V addressing and min/mag filters, mipmaps, copying existing albedo sampler state, unbinding, and resource ownership. It does not execute Vulkan or apply mixins. Simultaneous aliases of one texture with conflicting sampler states still need descriptor-level handling before full compatibility can be claimed.

## Render state and environment split

Pipeline replacement now has a version-specific implementation: 1.21.11 reconstructs `RenderSetup` with every original field, while 1.21.10 reuses the complete `CompositeState` and preserves buffer size, crumbling, and sort-on-upload flags. No render-state hook is disabled.

Environment reads (moon phase, sun/moon angles, cloud height, sky color) have a shared boundary with implementations for both APIs. Legacy texture slots now target the actual `RenderSystem` cache, rather than a nonexistent VulkanMod field. Legacy compressed terrain element names and camera/resource-key getters are adapted.

After resolving those missing types, javac checked method bodies and found further API differences. The current probe reports 11 errors: acquisition state, newer option uniforms, section UBO layout, and post-input filtering. End-to-end rendering remains unverified. The legacy cloud-height fallback of 192 for dimensions without clouds also requires parity validation.

## Main compilation milestone

The 1.21.10 main sources now compile. Old terrain has no section UBO, and its separate `CUTOUT_MIPPED` layer routes to the cutout shader family. Acquisition is tracked from the actual native result (success or suboptimal), so flush submissions retain their semaphore dependency without calling the absent `SwapChain.isAcquired` method. CPU acquisition-result checks are included with `checkPortAdapters`.

Post-input filtering has a version boundary; old custom post textures receive texture-owned filter state. The legacy option uniforms report no RGSS/anisotropic filtering and a one-millisecond fade interval because no section-fade setting exists. These values and the legacy environment adapter still require visual/reference checks.

The first full `build` attempt proceeds past main compilation but fails on nine graphics-harness errors (game rules, acquisition diagnostics, and environment/cloud APIs). Tests, remapped distributable checks, mixin application, and rendering still need validation for this target. Two depth-clear injection mapping warnings are unresolved.

## First complete build and live startup

`build validateShaders -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.10` passed after adapting the graphics harness and backend contract checks. The remapped mod JAR passes `python scripts/verify_jar.py --target 1.21.10`; negative checks reject mismatched Minecraft and VulkanMod versions. The default 1.21.11 `build validateShaders` also passes.

The old VulkanMod backend uses traditional render passes, so the dynamic-rendering format hook is now separate and only included for 1.21.11. Its legacy contract checks require the actual native render-pass binding instead. The old atlas hook targets `AbstractTexture.setUseMipmaps`, preserves filtering during terrain submission, and restores the original state afterwards.

An isolated 1.21.10 vanilla-baseline world was prepared with Mojang data version 4556. Live startup exposed and fixed a forbidden plain interface inside the mixin package, then an acquisition reset before nested frame completion. The next run entered the test world but stopped when applying `VulkanCloudRendererMixin`: the old cloud render entry point takes `(ClientLevel, float, float, double, double, double)`, unlike the 1.21.11 signature. No rendered-frame pass has been recorded yet.

## First rendered shader fixture (1.21.10)

The cloud-preparation injection now takes only `CallbackInfo`; it uses no native method arguments and works with both cloud render signatures. A bytecode regression check covers that contract.

The isolated RTX 3070 vanilla-baseline run passed and shut down with zero tracked buffers, images, and pipelines before allocator destruction. The subsequent terrain MRT run initially exposed the missing legacy vertex UBO layout and a gap at descriptor binding 2. The terrain adapter now recognizes the old native shader and retains its UBO fields. The existing sparse descriptor-layout implementation also handles non-SSBO pipelines, so gaps no longer index past VulkanMod's layout array.

`build/targets/1.21.10/reports/geometry-mrt-sparse/result.json` reports passed: 222800 active magenta pixels, zero after disabling shaders, cursor ungrabbed, all audio categories muted, and the null audio backend. Shutdown tracked resource counts reach zero. This is one synthetic terrain fixture, not full-pack or cross-GPU certification; no Khronos validation layer was enabled in this run.

A pinned 1.21.9 profile now shares the legacy adapters and uses Fabric API 0.134.1+1.21.9. Its build and initial terrain MRT fixture now pass; wider compatibility remains unverified.

## 1.21.9 validation milestone

The 1.21.9 profile passes `build validateShaders` and distributable verification. A real RTX 3070 terrain MRT run with Khronos validation and synchronization checks enabled passed: 223176 active marker pixels and zero after disabling shaders. The final process log has no `VUID-`, `Validation Error`, `SYNC-`, or device-lost diagnostics, including teardown.

Validation first exposed a retained GL-style framebuffer cache and overlapping asynchronous texture uploads in VulkanMod 0.6.6. Legacy-only adapters now retire cached framebuffers without destroying borrowed texture attachments and serialize transfer writes before image copies. Backend bytecode tests check the cleanup ownership flag and upload injection point.

Always rerun the fixture preparation command before a smoke run: a successful previous fixture disables shaders and persists that selection. A zero Gradle exit code alone does not establish fixture success; inspect `result.json` and the full validation log.

A pinned 1.21.5 profile has been added for the next API port probe (VulkanMod 0.5.6, Fabric API 0.128.2+1.21.5). It has not yet been compiled or adapted.

## Latest regression verification

After the cloud, legacy terrain, descriptor layout, upload synchronization, and shutdown fixes:

- 1.21.11: `build validateShaders` passed; 695 JUnit tests, no failures/errors/skips.
- 1.21.10: `build validateShaders` passed; 698 JUnit tests, no failures/errors/skips, plus adapter self-checks.
- 1.21.9: `build validateShaders` passed; 698 JUnit tests, no failures/errors/skips, plus adapter self-checks.
- All three remapped JARs pass the distributable verifier with their exact target dependencies.
- Each shader validation run processed 106 example sources and directly compiled 125 Vulkan SPIR-V stages.

The latest 1.21.9 synchronized MRT fixture passed with zero validation diagnostics through device destruction. The previous retained render-pass/framebuffer leak is no longer reported. The 1.21.5 dependency/compilation probe has started. Full shader/feature matrices and a fresh graphics regression of the default 1.21.11 backend are still required; these checks do not certify all 1.21.x releases.

## Older API boundary and UI adapter

The default 1.21.11 terrain MRT regression passed on RTX 3070 with core/synchronization validation: 222865 active marker pixels, zero after disabling, and no validation diagnostics through process exit. This verifies the shared descriptor-layout change on the dynamic-rendering backend too.

The 1.21.5 probe confirms that `GpuTextureView`, `GpuBufferSlice`, modern render-submission classes, and the current VulkanMod GLSL parser do not exist there. Its backend uses the older `GlslConverter`, immediate uniforms, and texture-owned bindings. Shared legacy name adapters now apply, with a separate 1.21.5 source-override layer; the full mod does not compile yet.

Pack-list rows now have a small version boundary for rendering and mouse events. The 1.21.5 adapter compiles against the pinned Minecraft dependency and passes click-timing checks with `checkLegacyUiAdapters -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.5`. This is not a verified full menu or renderer port. Texture/buffer views, shader conversion, immediate entity/block/item hooks, post passes, and the remaining screens still require adaptation.

## Diagnostic scope and broader legacy graphics checks

A full 1.21.5 diagnostic compile (with the compiler error limit raised for this probe) reports 587 errors, including 248 in `VeyraRenderer` and 54 in `VeyraShadowRenderer`. This confirms the port needs real replacements for the unavailable rendering APIs; removing a few imports is insufficient. Diagnostics are saved under `build/targets/1.21.5/compile-diagnostics.json` locally.

The updated shared pack-row code still compiles for 1.21.11 and 1.21.9. The old row adapter is a separate tested source override, while the full 1.21.5 mod remains unbuildable.

PBR smoke resource packs now declare format 69.0 for 1.21.9/10 and retain 75.0 for 1.21.11. A broader 1.21.9 GPU check sequence has started for entity MRT, begin fragments, compute color targets, cloud model-view, animated normal atlases, and water/shadow terrain depth. It prepares each fixture afresh and requires both a passed `result.json` and zero full-process validation diagnostics. Results are incomplete until the sequence finishes.

## Broader 1.21.9 fixture results and 1.21.5 texture bridge

All six representative 1.21.9 fixtures now pass their pixel checks and full-process Khronos/core/synchronization logs: `entity-mrt`, `begin-fragment-stage`, `compute-color-target`, `cloud-model-view`, `normal-atlas-animated`, and `water-shadow-depth-terrain`. This remains representative coverage, not the complete shader-pack/feature matrix.

The water/shadow fixture exposed two independent defects: native render-pass dependencies omitted late depth writes, and the shadow renderer skipped the old `CUTOUT_MIPPED` opaque layer. Legacy render-pass dependencies now include early/late depth stages and depth-write access; shadow geometry enumerates every non-translucent backend layer before taking the opaque-depth snapshot. Its rerun passed with 226902 active marker pixels, zero disabled pixels, and zero validation diagnostics. Targeted shadow-layer/backend contract tests also pass. Resource generation now explicitly tracks build-script changes so new mixins cannot remain absent behind a stale Gradle output.

The 1.21.5 port has tested borrowed texture-range types and a VulkanMod texture factory. Checks cover mip dimensions, bounds/overflow rejection, unsupported texture backends, independent view closure, and retaining ownership of the underlying texture. Native subresource binding and conversion of old render-pass callbacks are still incomplete; the full 1.21.5 mod remains unsupported. The adapter checks are wired into that target's eventual `check` task.

The same six GPU fixtures also pass on 1.21.10, with zero core/synchronization validation diagnostics through process exit. Local evidence is in `build/targets/1.21.10/graphics-matrix.json` and the referenced full process logs.

The 1.21.5 sampler now validates a borrowed view before mutating native texture sampling state. Adapter checks verify that unsupported mip subranges and closed views neither change the texture nor reach the native render pass. Its native shader-texture cache override uses `GpuTexture[]`, matching that version's API. These are port components; they do not establish a working 1.21.5 client.

## 1.21.5 render-target view lifecycle

The old render target stores textures directly. `LegacyRenderTargetMixin` supplies cached borrowed views through an interface outside the mixin package; generated 1.21.5 call sites use this interface rather than nonexistent native view getters. Checks cover color/depth separation, stable identity, attachment replacement, closed-view recreation, supplied-view validation, and cleanup without destroying borrowed textures. The mixin replaces the modern field accessor only for 1.21.5, and generated resources contain the replacement.

The pack-row version boundary also handles issue tooltips: deferred tooltips on modern versions and the old `renderTooltip` API on 1.21.5. The old adapter checks and default 1.21.11 compilation pass. The latest full 1.21.5 diagnostic compile still fails with 468 errors; the older diagnostic JSON is historical, while `build/target-1.21.5-full-diagnostics.log` contains the current compile. No 1.21.5 runtime compatibility is claimed.

## 1.21.5 texture allocation and descriptor lookup

Color-target metadata allocation now goes through `VeyraTargetTextures`: modern versions retain the previous usage flags (or attachment/sampleable defaults), while 1.21.5 calls its native five-argument texture allocator. Native image format replacement and resource ownership stay in the existing renderer code. Adapter checks cover both missing/existing previous attachments, dimensions, format and mip count without destroying either texture.

Old VulkanMod exposes image descriptors as a list, without the modern name lookup method. The 1.21.5 source adapter now performs exact name lookup against that list, preserving native descriptor identity, binding, slot and storage/sampler properties. Empty and missing/case-mismatched names are checked. These checks pass against the pinned old dependency; the full port still fails with 421 compilation errors. This does not certify an old client or shader-pack run.

## Separate distributables and legacy sampler encoding

Each Minecraft version must have its own remapped JAR with an `+mc<version>` suffix and exact dependency metadata for manual Modrinth upload. Build CI now runs isolated jobs for the currently buildable 1.21.9, 1.21.10 and 1.21.11 targets, with separate mod artifacts and test reports. Incomplete ports must join this matrix only when they build and pass their gates. No Modrinth upload has been performed.

The 1.21.5 native sampler bridge maps clamp, linear filtering, mipmap usage and linear mip interpolation to the old cache's byte flags, with range validation before narrowing maximum LOD. Checks cover all eight clamp/filter/mip combinations and invalid LOD bounds. A version-only accessor supplies the native sampler assignment absent from the old image API, including caller-owned samplers for raw textures; it does not take sampler ownership. Adapter checks and resource generation pass. Full 1.21.5 compilation remains incomplete with 406 errors, and no GPU sampler claim is made for this version.

## 1.21.5 post-pass input binding

The 1.21.5 post-input interface now implements the old `bindTo` contract: validate the borrowed view, apply filter/mipmap state, bind `<input>Sampler`, and upload `<input>Size` as an immediate float uniform. Mipmapped snapshots explicitly request mipmaps; ordered wrappers preserve that property. Name and texture resolution also handle native target/texture inputs, with a clear error for a missing target or unsupported input implementation.

Pinned-dependency self-checks verify native binding names, dimensions, filtering/mip state, borrowed ownership and rejection of a closed view before native calls. Native target name lookup and missing-target handling pass too. The full 1.21.5 compile still fails with 396 errors: post-pass construction/uniform conversion and full runtime rendering are not ported yet. Default 1.21.11 compilation and the 1.21.9 adapter regression are checked separately.

## 1.21.5 uniform packing

The old game lacks `UniformValue` and the std140 writer/size calculator. Version-only internal implementations now support the scalar, vec2, vec4 and mat4 values used by the renderer; the shared packed vec3/ivec2/ivec3 implementations use these types through the source adapter. No classes are injected into Mojang's packages.

Checks cover exact independent byte offsets in a 144-byte mixed block, integer bit preservation, vec3/scalar sharing, matrix column order, zeroed alignment padding, a nonzero buffer start, repeated reads, overflow/bounds rejection and stack-backed native-order storage. Matrix writes use scalar access rather than JOML's unsafe buffer path (the first heap-buffer test exposed an access violation with that path). The check task reuses the existing LWJGL core native dependency for its stack-allocation check.

`checkLegacyTextureAdapters checkLegacyUiAdapters -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.5` passes. The full diagnostic compile now fails with 288 errors. Post-pass construction, GPU buffer upload and older rendering hooks remain incomplete; successful CPU packing is not evidence of a working old renderer.

## 1.21.5 buffer backend boundary

The sky-horizon vertex allocation now uses a version-specific helper: modern usage flags are retained, while 1.21.5 requests `VERTICES` with `STATIC_WRITE` through the supported native allocator. A pinned-API check verifies the uploaded byte range, label, returned handle and caller ownership. Default 1.21.11 and 1.21.9 compilation pass after this shared caller change. The full old port remains unbuildable (286 compiler errors).

Important native limitation: the old `BufferType.UNIFORM` enum exists, but `VkGpuBuffer`'s constructor switch does not support it. A direct translation of the modern uniform-buffer allocation would compile and then throw at runtime. The adapter self-check records this native contract. Uniform blocks must be connected through the old VulkanMod UBO/descriptor path or a real extension to its backend before old post-processing can be enabled; do not substitute an unsupported enum call to reduce compiler errors.

## Native compute uniform blocks

Compute UBO construction now uses `VeyraUniformBlocks`. Modern profiles retain named blocks and explicit global-buffer selection; 1.21.5 uses its native `AlignedStruct.Builder`, where a null per-block buffer selects the frame-managed global buffer. Tests build an actual native old UBO and verify binding, compute stage, total byte size and field word offsets. They execute native `UBO.update` into bounded stack memory and verify every supplied word, untouched padding and refreshed supplier data on a second update.

The 1.21.5 adapter checks pass, and its full diagnostic compile remains incomplete with 282 errors. Default 1.21.11 and 1.21.9 compilation pass. The shared change passes the 1.21.10 compute-color-target GPU check with zero full-process validation diagnostics. The setup-stage uniform (`viewWidth`/`viewHeight`) pixel and resize assertions pass, but its full log reports two WRITE_AFTER_PRESENT hazards and two non-acquired-swapchain-image diagnostics during resize. That fixture is NOT a clean graphics pass; the legacy resize/acquisition path needs fixing. Reports: `build/targets/1.21.10/reports/ubo-adapter` and `ubo-uniforms`; logs: `build/ubo-adapter-graphics.log` and `build/ubo-uniforms-graphics.log`. This validates the modern regression and old native CPU copy path, not old GPU execution or complete 1.21.5 support.

## Acquired-image wait during resize

The resize/discard branch of native `Renderer.submitFrame` submits recorded swapchain transitions without adding the image-acquisition semaphore. The existing adapter only widened the stage mask of a wait that already existed. It now adds the missing acquisition wait at ALL_COMMANDS, preserving upload waits, command buffers, signal semaphores and extension chains. This applies to the modern and legacy submission adapters. Already-consumed acquisition still uses the separate removal path.

A new test fails on the previous implementation for empty/upload-only wait lists and passes after the fix; all five submission helper tests pass for 1.21.10 and the default backend. The fresh 1.21.10 setup-stage GPU rerun passes uniforms and resize with 360000 active marker pixels, zero disabled pixels and zero full-process validation diagnostics, including teardown. Report: `build/targets/1.21.10/reports/resize-acquire-fixed/result.json`; log: `build/resize-acquire-fixed-graphics.log`. Keep the failed `ubo-uniforms` run as negative evidence. The default 1.21.11 setup-stage GPU rerun also passes the resize assertion with zero full-process validation diagnostics. Evidence: `build/reports/resize-acquire-fixed/result.json` and `build/default-resize-acquire-fixed-graphics.log`.

## 1.21.5 native image descriptor constructor

Veyra-owned custom-image and entity-normal descriptors now share a small native constructor boundary. Modern backends receive their original Vulkan descriptor type; 1.21.5 maps combined image samplers/storage images to the native boolean constructor and rejects unsupported descriptor types. Native storage-image property reads use the old field API on that target.

Pinned-dependency checks verify binding, texture slot, sampled/storage type, initial image layout, sampler usage and invalid-type rejection. Modern image-binding/backend contract regression tests pass for 1.21.11 and 1.21.9. The full 1.21.5 diagnostic compile still fails with 276 errors. Native graphics pipeline conversion, post-pass UBO integration and a full old client run remain required.

## 1.21.5 native texture slots

Generated old callers now use `LegacyTextureSlots`, which binds the underlying native texture while retaining the supplied borrowed view's identity. Reads compare the actual native slot with the cached texture, so vanilla replacements invalidate stale metadata. The cache preserves closed-view identity for existing cleanup comparisons, does not close caller views/textures, and uses the same capacity as the expanded native slot array.

Checks cover the highest valid slot, vanilla replacements, stable identity, closed-view cleanup, null/unbind, wrong-backend rejection, invalid indices and native-setter failure. Validation occurs before native calls; cache state changes only after the native setter succeeds. The old texture/UI adapter tasks pass. Full 1.21.5 compilation remains incomplete with 258 errors. Native render-pass hook signatures still need overrides: the old hooks accept `GpuTexture`, and `ExtendedRenderPipeline.getPipeline` returns the broader `Pipeline` type. No old runtime claim is made.

## 1.21.5 render-pass hook overrides

Old `VkRenderPassMixin` callbacks now accept native textures, wrap entity albedo as a borrowed full-range view for PBR lookup, and bind depth/lightmap images through the old texture API. The old lightmap getter is `getTarget`. Graphics-only hooks guard the broader `Pipeline` return type before using it as `GraphicsPipeline`. Post-input code retains responsibility for mipmap/filter state before calling the texture-only native sampler API.

The old encoder override targets the no-label `createRenderPass(GpuTexture, OptionalInt, GpuTexture, OptionalDouble)` overload and passes underlying textures for shadow attachments. Its swapchain-clear reacquisition hook uses HEAD: the old clear method lacks the newer `Renderer.isRecording` invocation injection point and instead clears the current attachments. Arbitrary-target clear semantics of this older backend remain part of the unfinished runtime port.

Shared selector constants are checked against actual pinned dependency bytecode, including render-pass creation, sampler binding, pipeline setup, lightmap access and color-clear signatures. Texture/UI/native-hook checks pass, and the full diagnostic compile no longer reports errors in these two mixins. The mod still fails to compile with 251 other errors; mixin application and complete old client behavior remain unverified.

## 1.21.5 atlas and dimension APIs

The old block-atlas binding uses its native texture, retaining texture-owned sampler state and existing readiness errors. The modern `SamplerInfo` helper is not part of this target: the old terrain hook does not use that API. `WorldRenderer.renderSectionLayer` takes Minecraft `RenderType` on 1.21.5, so depth/translucency interception converts it through native `TerrainRenderType.get`. Atlas sharp filtering is applied just before native `VTextureSelector.bindShaderTextures`, retaining the layer's selected mipmap state and restoring the original sampler at return. The later `AbstractTexture.setUseMipmaps` injection point does not exist in this backend.

Cloud height comes from the old dimension effects API. Adapter checks exercise Overworld/Nether/End effects and custom finite heights; nonfinite no-cloud values retain the shared 192 fallback rather than propagating NaN into shader uniforms. The native terrain method descriptor is checked against the pinned backend. Texture/UI/hook/environment checks pass, and the latest full 1.21.5 diagnostic compile still fails with 245 errors. Runtime terrain filtering, shader-pack parity and native mixin application remain unverified until the old renderer builds and runs.

## 1.21.5 pipeline construction lifecycle

The old source adapter resolves `PipelineManager` in its earlier package and reads the native public terrain-format field where the later getter does not exist. A dedicated graphics-pipeline mixin uses the old two-SPIR-V shader-module construction call and nested descriptor-set array type. It retains failure cleanup, shadow front-face reversal, MRT blend/write masks and render-pass cache retirement.

Native bytecode checks verify both pipeline-manager entry points and the private shader-module descriptor. A construction-order check confirms layouts exist before module creation and descriptor pools are created afterward, matching the cleanup hook's assumptions. UBO labels in sparse-layout diagnostics now use a version boundary; old unnamed blocks are identified by binding. Functional shader-block name handling is not replaced with diagnostic labels.

The old adapter/UI checks pass, and the default 1.21.11 backend contract regression passes after the shared diagnostic change. Full 1.21.5 compilation remains incomplete with 234 errors; native shader-construction failure cleanup and MRT behavior still require an actual old client run.

## 1.21.5 sampled depth views

The depth-view override uses the old 2D image-view factory and old native texture constructor, retaining the source format, dimensions and mip count. The alias borrows the depth image; its view handle still retires through the native frame-fence queue before the alias registry entry is detached and closed.

View release has an independent once-only guard, so an externally closed texture alias does not skip native view retirement. CPU ownership checks cover ordinary/repeated closure, an already-closed alias and cleanup-error propagation without repeated cleanup. Native factory/constructor descriptors are verified against the pinned dependency. These checks exercise the closure wrapper and its callback, not real Vulkan allocation/free operations.

Texture/UI/native-hook checks pass. The full 1.21.5 diagnostic compile remains incomplete with 230 errors. Depth sampling, fence retirement and borrowed native image survival need GPU verification once the target builds.

## 1.21.5 immediate entity and item rendering

Version overrides now wrap native entity, block-entity, hand-item and item-stack rendering calls with the existing identifier contexts and `finally` cleanup. Block-entity shadow culling still uses the original block position/state. Item-source capture uses the old `LivingEntity` owner signature, retains clear/reset behavior, and covers all rendered item layers through the old `ItemStackRenderState.render` entry point.

The old `BufferSource.getBuffer` hook selects the existing identifier-specific render-type/pipeline variant while the context is active, so the batch can retain that pipeline after the context ends. It replaces the later submit-node routing on this target. Only 1.21.5 excludes the obsolete item-layer invoker and `SubmitNodeCollectionMixin`; modern target configuration retains them. The old render-type factory/state accessor also compiles in the standalone adapter check.

Native method selectors and exact entity/block-entity redirect call sites are checked against pinned bytecode. Adapter checks pass and generated source/config isolation is checked. The latest full compile stops during symbol resolution with 70 errors, including absent modern GUI, submit-node text and GLSL parser APIs. This is a compiler-phase diagnostic, not a complete count of remaining port work; later body errors can reappear once those types are resolved. Actual batched entity/item/sign-text ID parity, nested contexts and exceptions still need client/GPU verification before claiming 1.21.5 support.

## Fresh 1.21.10 build after background failure notification

A delayed command notification reported compiling raw `src/main/java` against 1.21.10 dependencies. The current quoted target invocation uses generated port sources and does not reproduce that failure. Fresh `compileJava` and `build validateShaders -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.10` pass; the remapped target JAR passes `verify_jar.py --target 1.21.10`. Logs: `build/current-1.21.10-compile.log` and `build/current-1.21.10-build.log`. The truncated historical command is insufficient to establish why it selected raw sources.

PowerShell wrappers must propagate `$LASTEXITCODE` from Gradle before running log-viewing commands; otherwise the wrapper can report exit zero while its Gradle log says BUILD FAILED. A wrapper exit code alone is not evidence of a successful build.

## 1.21.5 world/GUI frame boundary

The old game renders GUI directly from `GameRenderer` instead of the later `GuiRenderer`. A target override completes the shader frame before the first GUI depth clear, marks the following GUI stage, preserves world depth before hand rendering, and restores GUI/hand flags through `finally` wrappers. The existing phase cursor retains the end-of-frame fallback without running composite twice.

The newer `GuiRendererMixin` is excluded only from 1.21.5 source/configuration. Native method and bytecode-order checks verify world rendering precedes the selected first depth clear and GUI drawing follows it; hand-render parameters and depth-clear signatures are checked too. Texture/UI/hook checks pass. The full old compile currently stops at 61 symbol-resolution errors; this is not a complete inventory of later body errors. Actual GUI pixels, exception cleanup and world/hand depth behavior require client validation when the target builds.

## Text context identity independent of submit-node classes

`TextSubmissionContexts` now accepts opaque identity keys: its algorithm never reads submission fields and no longer imports a Minecraft submit-node class. Renderer forwarding methods use the same opaque keys/list, while modern hooks still pass their native text submissions. Identity-map behavior, nested ownership and removal remain unchanged.

The old immediate buffer-routing hook captures the sign/block pipeline while the block-entity context is active, so 1.21.5 excludes the later `TextFeatureRendererMixin`. Modern profiles retain it. Standalone old checks cover equal-but-distinct keys, nested/missing contexts, one-shot consumption and identity-specific clearing. Existing native-text context and entity-ID regression tests pass on 1.21.9 and 1.21.11; generated configuration isolation also passes.

Full 1.21.5 compilation stops at 46 symbol-resolution errors, with GLSL parsing, LevelRenderState/shadow feature submission and post-pass APIs still unfinished. This is not a total count of remaining errors or evidence of old text GPU parity. Verify actual sign text and entity/item ID batching after the complete old client builds.

## Shadow entity submission by backend version

`VeyraShadowEntities` now owns version-specific entity/block-entity submission and modern feature-dispatcher lifetime. The common shadow renderer retains its matrices, feature flag, pass ownership and `finally` restoration. Modern behavior retains entity/player deduplication, frustum culling, submission clearing and dispatcher release. The old backend renders interpolated entities with native packed light and block entities through its coordinate-based API, then flushes the borrowed Minecraft buffers; it allocates no feature dispatcher.

The old LevelRenderer hook now uses its real render signature, captures current fog color at `FogRenderer.computeFogColor`, and runs begin compute before shadow/prepare at frame-graph execution. It no longer needs the absent `levelRenderState` field accessor. Native render/fog/frame-graph descriptors and call sites are checked against pinned bytecode. The full compile now exposes 27 remaining framebuffer/image/game API mismatches (`build/legacy-storage-backend-full-compile.log`). The old mod still does not build.

Deferred-clear bookkeeping now goes through `VeyraTargetTextures.markCleared`: modern VulkanMod clears its `needsClear` flag after the explicit native clear; 0.5.6 has no deferred-clear flag. The invalid accessor is excluded from both old sources and mixin metadata. Old adapter/resource checks and the default target texture regression pass. This does not establish old post-processing or graphics compatibility.

### Native 1.21.5 pipeline configuration and terrain positions

The old profile now uses its own configuration/builder adapter at Veyra call sites. It builds actual VulkanMod UBOs, delegates push constants and shader compilation to the native builder, and keeps explicit bindings/texture slots for additional graphics images and storage buffers. Duplicate bindings are rejected. Integer vectors retain their raw bytes in native byte-copy vector fields; the old effective fog range supplies the split fog fields. Native mixin constructor signatures remain unchanged.

CPU checks verify old terrain/cloud JSON, sampler slots against the native method bytecode, integer bit patterns (including negative/NaN-like patterns), matrix/scalar offsets, per-update values, push-constant bytes and image descriptor types. Classpath shader loading handles native JAR resources without relying on a mounted ZIP filesystem. The old GLSL converter and post-processing uniform integration remain unfinished; a full client run is still required.

The terrain bridge now handles 0.5.6's homogeneous position function, preserving world-space coordinates for shadow and material inputs. Both compressed/uncompressed shader fixtures compile to SPIR-V; 158 shader adapter tests pass on each of 1.21.9 and 1.21.11. The actual pinned 0.5.6 terrain source also compiles after adaptation, with unused native light/fog include directives removed for standalone glslang validation. `build/pinned-legacy-terrain-check.json` records source/output hashes. This is shader-level validation, not runtime support for 1.21.5.

### Post projection and cloud block alignment

`VeyraPostProjection` now bridges post-pass scheduling: modern versions retain the borrowed cached GPU projection, while 1.21.5 uses native PostChain's orthographic matrix and four-argument `PostPass.addToFrame`. Each old frame owns a matrix snapshot. The obsolete old ShaderManager buffer accessor is excluded from source and mixin metadata. CPU tests cover clip-space boundaries, invalid viewports, frame ownership and native delegation. Default backend tests pass; a fresh 1.21.10 water-shadow-depth-entity graphics run passes with zero full-process Vulkan core/synchronization diagnostics (`build/post-projection-graphics.log`).

The 1.21.5 Veyra cloud pipeline now normalizes its old native cloud uniform block and vertex header to the same ColorModulator/FogCloudsEnd layout used by the shared adapters. Binding 1 is visible to both shader stages; native MVP/ModelOffset and the vertex body remain intact. Pinned source/schema tests and standalone glslang compilation pass (`build/pinned-legacy-cloud-check.json`). Adapter checks compile the generated overlay sources, so build-filter changes are covered too. These checks do not establish runtime support.

### Legacy GLSL conversion and named post parameters

The old profile now targets its real `GlslConverter`. Veyra's generated flat UBO declarations are lowered to named uniforms, including references through `veyraParameters` instances, and the native converter builds its merged UBO and stage interfaces. Comment masking and function-parameter aliases prevent old line parsing from consuming declarations inside comments or multiline `in/out/inout` parameters. Arrays/struct UBO fields are explicitly rejected; Veyra's generated parameter blocks use the supported flat scalar/vector/matrix forms. Native converted vertex/fragment outputs compile to SPIR-V through shaderc.

Direct SSBO preparation retains real storage declarations and adds their native descriptors to the builder. Native storage-image placeholder replacement, declaration restoration and normal texture slot reservation remain connected. Old integer vector fields use native byte-copy vector layouts and integer suppliers. The obsolete modern Lexer/UniformBlock hooks are excluded only for this old implementation. Pinned bytecode checks cover the replacement hook signatures and redirect call counts; complete old-client mixin application remains untested.

`VeyraPostUniforms` preserves modern GPU uniform-buffer creation, updates and cleanup. The old override uses native named-uniform callbacks, with snapshots packed before scheduling, and preserves integer bit patterns and matrix order. Its IVEC2 values use the available native IVEC3 CPU storage with a padding word outside the shader field. Checks exercise actual old uniform storage plus updates, queued frame snapshots and close behavior (`build/legacy-named-uniform-check.log`). Default and 1.21.9 backend/shader adapter tests pass. The default 1.21.11 custom-uniforms graphics fixture also passes with zero full-process core/synchronization diagnostics (`build/default-named-uniforms-graphics.log`).

Remaining 1.21.5 compilation work is now visible inside method bodies: framebuffer/depth APIs, image shape/upload signatures and old gameplay/window APIs. Full mod compilation and graphics validation are still required.

### Native uniform metadata and fragment outputs

Original per-field source types are now retained across shader stages and connected to actual EGlProgram storage before native UBO suppliers are installed. Installation adds only missing fields, preserves existing storage and sampler lists, rejects incompatible types before changing the program, and uses native IVEC3 CPU storage for IVEC2's two meaningful words. CPU checks exercise real EGlProgram/Uniform buffers through native UBO byte copies, including matrices, integer bit patterns and subsequent updates. Modern block declarations and uniform-description copying retain their existing behavior through the shared helper; 1.21.5 defers block metadata until shader conversion. Default and 1.21.9 helper/backend tests pass.

The old converter's fixed fragColor header is now replaced by the shader's actual named outputs. Bare outputs receive free locations; explicit locations and MRT declarations inside macros are preserved. Native converted named-output and sparse MRT shaders compile through shaderc, and duplicate locations are rejected. Split fog fields are lowered to the old effective FogStart/FogEnd range, with native deduplication checked across both stages. These CPU/compiler checks still require a complete old-client mixin and rendering run.

### Owned SSBO descriptors and compute lifecycle

Storage descriptors now use a versioned base: modern VulkanMod retains its owned slice, while 0.5.6 receives the real storage buffer handle/range directly. The old descriptor update path skips global uniform allocation/copies for SSBOs, uses zero storage dynamic offsets, tracks owned handles for cache invalidation, and reserves the actual storage-buffer/storage-image pool types. Dynamic offsets follow binding order, cached once per descriptor object. Global uniform capacity is checked before taking upload offsets, including after resize.

CPU checks exercise native Vulkan descriptor structs, owned handles/ranges, mixed pool types and a CPU-backed native UniformBuffer. A unit-only alignment fixture verifies mixed global/owned/storage offsets and resize behavior; hardware validation is still required for 1.21.5. Native pipeline accessors expose the actual backend methods and preserve graphics pipeline ownership.

An initial compute regression called Pipeline.bindDescriptorSets with a compute bind point, but that overload actually accepts a frame index and always binds GRAPHICS. The failure is retained in `build/default-storage-backend-graphics.log`. Compute now invokes the current frame's DescriptorSets.bindSets with COMPUTE explicitly, through an invoker for old protected access. The corrected default 1.21.11 compute-ssbo-only fixture passes with zero full-process Vulkan core/synchronization diagnostics (`build/default-storage-backend-fixed-graphics.log`). Default and 1.21.9 unit/backend checks pass and guard the bind-point contract. The old mixins still require a running 1.21.5 client after its remaining compilation errors are fixed.

The extraction passes default 1.21.11 backend/shadow-layer tests and 1.21.9 tests with isolated project history. Fresh synchronized GPU `water-shadow-depth-entity` fixtures pass on 1.21.10 (14123 active marker pixels) and 1.21.11 (14032), zero disabled pixels and zero full-process validation diagnostics. Reports: `build/targets/1.21.10/reports/shadow-entities-port` and `build/reports/shadow-entities-port`; logs: `build/shadow-entities-port-graphics.log` and `build/default-shadow-entities-port-graphics.log`. Old 1.21.5 shadow behavior remains unverified in a client.

The first concurrent default/1.21.9 regression exposed shared Gradle project-history cleanup deleting another target's class output. A 1.21.9 rerun with `--project-cache-dir .gradle/targets/1.21.9` passes. Subsequent isolated 1.21.5/10/11 runs retain all three modern class output directories. CI and the commands at the start of this document now select per-version project caches; the failed initial test run is retained in `build/legacy-shadow-entities-regression.log` rather than counted as passing evidence.

## 2026-11-09 handoff (Claude, after the Codex session hit its usage limit)

The Codex session stopped mid-1.21.4 probe. Fresh `build validateShaders` passes for 1.21.11, 1.21.10, 1.21.9 and 1.21.5 (logs `build/claude-verify-<target>.log`), and all four JARs pass `verify_jar.py`. The work was committed as a checkpoint on `feat/minecraft-1.21-multiversion`.

A full 1.21.4 `compileJava` with `-Xmaxerrs 100000` (`build/claude-1.21.4-full.log`) reports 432 symbol-resolution errors: `com.mojang.blaze3d.textures`, `RenderPipeline` and VulkanMod's `net.vulkanmod.render.engine` are absent. The 0.6.7+1.21.1 VulkanMod JAR has no `render/engine` package either. Pre-1.21.5 versions need a separate render-integration backend; they are documented as unsupported pending an owner decision. VulkanMod has no 1.21.6–1.21.8 release (Modrinth API, 2026-11-09).

The release workflow now builds, verifies and attaches the 1.21.10, 1.21.9 and 1.21.5 JARs alongside 1.21.11. README lists the per-version requirements.

## 2026-11-10: Minecraft 1.21.4 target

1.21.4 is now a supported target (VulkanMod 0.5.6, `RAtVo03C`). `src/minecraft1214/java` provides `dev.veyra.compat`, a stand-in for the 1.21.5 GPU abstraction on top of the 1.21.4 render-type/program path; `build.gradle` (`veyraLegacy4Line`) rewrites the shared sources to it. See `docs/MULTIVERSION.md` for the design.

Runtime faults found and fixed during GPU bring-up, all invisible to compilation:
- VulkanMod 1.21.4 numbers sampler slots by declaration order and remaps them in `CompiledShaderProgram.apply()`; Veyra programs get the 1.21.5 slot layout (`Legacy4UniformParserMixin`) and declare no samplers to the program.
- Vanilla pipelines have no name in this VulkanMod; `VeyraGeometryTargets` now tolerates that (shared fix).
- 1.21.4 program configs already define `ALPHA_CUTOUT`; pipeline defines are keyed by name.
- 1.21.5 merged per-render-type shaders (`core/entity`, `core/glint`, ...); classification compares through `VanillaPipelines.shaderIs`.
- The world pass's clear ran with no render pass bound and was dropped, so stale depth rejected every draw (entities, sky, GUI) with no validation error. Clears with nothing bound now target the main target, as OpenGL's default framebuffer.
- VulkanMod reads blend/depth/write state globally on this version; post passes apply their pipeline's state and restore defaults.
- Render-target creation/resize and attachment replacement left GL-emulation render passes open across copies and barriers; bindings are saved and restored. Barriers during input binding suspend the pass. Depth-stencil images get depth-only sampled views (destroyed with the image and at shutdown).

Evidence (RTX 3070, Vulkan core + synchronization validation, whole-process logs including shutdown): vanilla-baseline, entity-mrt, begin-fragment-stage, compute-color-target, compute-ssbo-only, cloud-model-view, normal-atlas-animated and water-shadow-depth-terrain all pass with zero diagnostics. Logs `build/final-1.21.4-<fixture>.log`, reports `build/targets/1.21.4/reports/final-<fixture>/result.json`. `build validateShaders -PtargetMinecraft=1.21.4` passes 706 tests and `checkLegacy4PipelineAdapters` (`build/claude-build-1.21.4.log`); `verify_jar.py --target 1.21.4` passes. Regression builds for 1.21.11/10/9/5 pass (`build/claude-regress-<target>.log`). CI and the release workflow build, verify and attach the 1.21.4 JAR.

Still not targets: 1.21–1.21.3 (separate VulkanMod generations; 1.21.4's backend is the starting point) and 1.21.6–1.21.8 (no VulkanMod release).

## 2026-11-10: Minecraft 1.21.2 and 1.21.3 targets

Both pin VulkanMod 0.5.3 (`mr9uxFGT`, one JAR for both versions) and build on the 1.21.4 backend. `src/minecraft1213` and `veyraLegacy3Line` cover what changed; see `docs/MULTIVERSION.md`.

Faults found during bring-up:
- 0.5.3's compressed terrain vertex stores color before UV0 (as a normalized `vec4`). Veyra's extended format initially followed 0.5.6's order. That produced 12 `VUID-VkGraphicsPipelineCreateInfo-Input-08733` vertex-input mismatches in vanilla mode; the format and adapter markers now follow 0.5.3.
- 1.21.3 has no `VertexBuffer.drawWithRenderType`, so that 1.21.4 hook is excluded. The generated mixin list honours exclusions; an excluded-but-listed mixin crashed startup once.
- Animation metadata is read through `forEachFrame` rather than an accessor, so it also works in plain unit tests.

Evidence (RTX 3070, core + synchronization validation, whole-process logs): vanilla-baseline plus the seven release fixtures pass with zero diagnostics on 1.21.3 and on 1.21.2 (logs `build/final-1.21.{2,3}-<fixture>.log`). 706 tests, `checkLegacy4PipelineAdapters` and `validateShaders` pass on both; `verify_jar.py` passes for both JARs. Static mixin checks find no target differences between 1.21.2 and 1.21.3.

The sky-stage fixtures (`skybasic-vertex-execution`, `skytextured-stage-variants`) fail on 1.21.3, 1.21.4 and the released 1.21.5 alike: the shared renderer recognises the sky only by the 1.21.6+ `core/sky` shaders. This is documented as a limitation before 1.21.6. 1.21/1.21.1 (VulkanMod 0.6.7+1.21.1) remain unported.
